a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿l-Ins described the human practitioner’s role. This chapter describes the jinn operator’s role.

﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿The franchise promised you power. The Creator promises you forgiveness.

T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿he fitrah responds. The mode collapse is never total. The door is always open.
